- **esp32-arch-review**: Reviews ESP32 firmware architecture for FreeRTOS safety, memory and stack sizing, ISR rules, error handling, peripheral and pin safety, networking, storage, logging and build configuration, and returns a PASS / PASS WITH NOTES / FAIL report.
